iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfgiidom32uSdvPxmyr1b","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've converted `stencil3d.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt` to a hybrid MPI + CUDA + OpenMP program; no new files and the executable is still `stencil3d`. Its output matches the original bit for bit: Sum, Min/Max, the sample values, hash and validation result were identical in all 35 cases I tried, covering 7 grid shapes on 1, 2, 3, 4 and 7 ranks. That includes odd sizes, more ranks than z-planes, 0 iterations and degenerate grids like `-x 1`. I tested on this machine: 4× RTX 3090, OpenMPI 4.1.6. I couldn't test across several nodes.\n\n**Performance** (million cell updates per second; the original single-threaded CPU code does about 455):\n\n| GPUs | 512³ | 1024³ |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 26,200 | 26,300 |\n| 2 | 49,400 | 51,600 |\n| 4 | 67,000–76,000 | 99,800 (about 95% of linear) |\n\nAt 512³, 4 GPUs still lose some time to the halo exchange; on its own, the compute part scales to 94,000. The fix that helped most is listed under CUDA below.\n\n**How it's split up**\n- **MPI:** the grid is cut into slabs along z, one rank per GPU, and ranks pick their GPU by their rank within the node. Each step, neighbouring ranks swap one plane of edge data. This MPI build can't send straight from GPU memory and the 3090s can't copy directly to each other, so the data goes through pinned host memory. Ranks left without planes sit idle. The full grid is only gathered onto rank 0 when `-r` or `-v` is given.\n- **CUDA:**\n  - The grid is initialized on the GPU. The fixed outer boundary is written into both buffers once, so it never needs copying again.\n  - The kernel processes the grid in x/y tiles, with each thread stepping through a range of z.\n  - The planes next to a neighbouring rank are computed first, on a high-priority stream. Their exchange then runs while the rest of the slab is computed. The high priority roughly doubled 4-GPU throughput, because without it the edge work queued behind the big kernel.\n- **OpenMP:** the validation checks (NaN/Inf, min/max) on rank 0 run as parallel reductions.\n- **Exact division:** the 3090 is slow at double-precision maths, so the `/7.0` was the bottleneck. I replaced it with a multiply by 1/7 plus one correction step, which gives exactly the same rounding (Markstein's method). I also checked it against true division on 2.1×10¹⁰ random inputs with zero differences. It's about 30% faster.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** compiles the source as CUDA (GPU architecture auto-detected unless you set one) and requires MPI, OpenMP and the CUDA toolkit. Build and run as usual, e.g. `mpirun -np 4 ./stencil3d -x 1024`.\n\nThings that behave differently from the original:\n- Only rank 0 prints. The reported time now covers the whole parallel run, including waiting for the GPUs and MPI to finish.\n- Rank placement matters: GPUs 2 and 3 are attached to the second CPU socket, and `mpirun`'s default core binding puts their ranks on the first one. `--bind-to none` was faster in some runs, but most of the gap was within run-to-run noise.\n- I tried removing the per-iteration host waits by chaining the GPU streams directly; it was slightly slower, so I kept the simpler version."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1447,"cache_read_input_tokens":65394,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1447},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4a735670-64e3-4b8d-a560-c7a18dc87d6f","uuid":"215ba6d7-b5ca-4574-90d3-68cce1aa5efb","timestamp":"2026-10-04T06:25:53.830Z","request_id":"req_011CfgiidMiMaQiq9NV2v8mc"}

It's about 30% faster.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** compiles the source as CUDA (GPU architecture auto-detected unless you set one) and requires MPI, OpenMP and the CUDA toolkit. Build and run as usual, e.g. `mpirun -np 4 ./stencil3d -x 1024`.\n\nThings that behave differently from the original:\n- Only rank 0 prints. The reported time now covers the whole parallel run, including waiting for the GPUs and MPI to finish.\n- Rank placement matters: GPUs 2 and 3 are attached to the second CPU socket, and `mpirun`'s default core binding puts their ranks on the first one. `--bind-to none` was faster in some runs, but most of the gap was within run-to-run noise.\n- I tried removing the per-iteration host waits by chaining the GPU streams directly; it was slightly slower, so I kept the simpler version.","ttft_ms":2908,"type":"result","duration_ms":1286927,"uuid":"e0edc228-9bd2-4cbd-bea4-56def444335d","ttft_stream_ms":1569,"time_to_request_ms":141,"first_content_frame_ms":1569,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
